[sys] 📺 The Long Odds. Dice against the house champion — a smug alien who has never lost on camera. The dice are fair. The stakes are the game. Raise, hold, or walk — but the table is unavoidable.

People have been hurt in this one. Not always.

The lights come up. You can hear the crowd — millions of them, somewhere past the sky.

"ANTE," says Vex. "FIFTY. AND ANYTHING ELSE YOU'D LIKE TO LOSE."

Nobody's beaten Vex on camera. The veterans just say "don't think at the table." They won't say why.
